and the irreps obtained from them.

From the table, we see that the objects
{\phi_{\mathrm{a\rho}}}^{\!\!\!\!\!\!2\alpha}
and
{\xi_{\mathrm{c\rho}}}^{\!\!\!\!\!\!2\alpha}
transform in the same way. By coupling them together using the cross term
|{\phi_{\mathrm{a\rho}}}^{\!\!\!\!\!\!2\alpha}-\frac{r_{\!\phi_{\mathrm{a}}}r_{\!\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}}{r_{\!\xi_{\mathrm{c}}}r_{\!\rho_{\mathrm{s}}}}{\xi_{\mathrm{c\rho}}}^{\!\!\!\!\!\!2\alpha}|^{2}
, we can break
\tilde{T}_{\phi_{\mathrm{a}}}
. This term vanishes at the vevs
\langle\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rangle
and
\langle\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\rangle
(
.3
) if we assign

\langle\rho_{\mathrm{p}}\rangle=\frac{r_{\!\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}}{\sqrt{2}}(\varsigma,\bar{\varsigma})^{\scriptscriptstyle T},\qquad\langle\rho_{\mathrm{s}}\rangle=r_{\!\rho_{\mathrm{s}}}.

(216)

The stabilizers of
\langle\rho_{\mathrm{p}}\rangle
and
\langle\rho_{\mathrm{s}}\rangle
(
216
) are given in TABLE
10
. These stabilizers fully determine
\langle\rho_{\mathrm{p}}\rangle
and
\langle\rho_{\mathrm{s}}\rangle
up to their norms. Hence, symmetry arguments guarantee that they appear as stationary points of the potentials of
\rho_{\mathrm{p}}
and
\rho_{\mathrm{s}}
.

Let us construct the general renormalizable potential involving
\rho_{\mathrm{p}}
,
\rho_{\mathrm{q}}
,
\rho_{\mathrm{r}}
and
\rho_{\mathrm{s}}
. Towards this end, we define the group element
\mathbf{E}_{\rho}=\mathbf{X}_{\rho}^{2}
. As shown in TABLE
11
,
\rho_{\mathrm{p}}
and
\rho_{\mathrm{s}}
change sign under
\mathbf{E}_{\rho}
while all other flavons remain invariant. Also, under both
\mathbf{E}_{\xi_{\mathrm{c}}}
and
\mathbf{E}_{\phi_{\mathrm{a}}}
,
\rho_{\mathrm{p}}
changes sign while
\rho_{\mathrm{q}}
,
\rho_{\mathrm{r}}
and
\rho_{\mathrm{s}}
remain invariant. This implies that
\rho_{\mathrm{p}}
and
\rho_{\mathrm{s}}
can appear only quadratically in the cross terms in the potential of
\rho_{\mathrm{p}}
,
\rho_{\mathrm{q}}
,
\rho_{\mathrm{r}}
and
\rho_{\mathrm{s}}
. These quadratic products are given by

\displaystyle\rho_{\mathrm{p}}\!\otimes\!\rho_{\mathrm{p}}\!

\displaystyle=\!|\rho_{\mathrm{p}}|^{2}\!\oplus{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!\!2\alpha}\!\oplus\!{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!\!2\beta}\!\oplus{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!\!2\gamma}\!\oplus{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!\!2\delta}\!\oplus\!{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!\!2\epsilon}\!\oplus{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!\!2\zeta},\!\!\!

(217)

\displaystyle\rho_{\mathrm{s}}\!\otimes\rho_{\mathrm{s}}\!

\displaystyle=\!|\rho_{\mathrm{s}}|^{2}\!\oplus{\rho_{\mathrm{s}}}^{\!\!\!2\alpha}\!\oplus{\rho_{\mathrm{s}}}^{\!\!\!2\beta},

(218)

where

\displaystyle{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!\!2\alpha}

\displaystyle={\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}_{1}^{\!\!*}{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}_{1}-{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}_{2}^{\!\!*}{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}_{2},

\displaystyle{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!\!2\beta}

\displaystyle={\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}_{1}^{\!\!*}{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}_{2},

(219)

\displaystyle{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!\!2\gamma}

\displaystyle=\text{re}({\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}_{1}{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}_{2}),

\displaystyle{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!\!2\delta}

\displaystyle=\text{im}({\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}_{1}{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}_{2}),

(220)

\displaystyle{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!\!2\epsilon}

\displaystyle={\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}_{1}^{\!\!*2}+{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}_{2}^{2},

\displaystyle{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!\!2\zeta}

\displaystyle={\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}_{1}^{\!\!*2}-{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}_{2}^{2},

(221)

\displaystyle{\rho_{\mathrm{s}}}^{\!\!\!2\alpha}

\displaystyle=\text{re}({\rho_{\mathrm{s}}}^{\!\!2}),

\displaystyle{\rho_{\mathrm{s}}}^{\!\!\!2\beta}

\displaystyle=\text{im}({\rho_{\mathrm{s}}}^{\!\!2}).

(222)

How these objects transform is given in TABLE
15
.

Using
\rho_{\mathrm{p}}
, we construct

\breve{\mathcal{V}}_{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}

=c_{81}\,m^{2}|\rho_{\mathrm{p}}|^{2}+c_{82}|\rho_{\mathrm{p}}|^{4}

(223)

=k_{67}(|\rho_{\mathrm{p}}|^{2}-r_{\!\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}^{2})^{2}-k_{67}r_{\!\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}^{4},

(224)

\mathcal{V}_{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}

=\breve{\mathcal{V}}_{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}+c_{83}({\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!\!2\alpha})^{2}+c_{84}({\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!\!2\delta})^{2}

(225)

\displaystyle\text{\small$=\breve{\mathcal{V}}_{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}+k_{68}({\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!\!2\alpha})^{2}+k_{69}({\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!\!2\delta})^{2}$}.

(226)

\breve{\mathcal{V}}_{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}
has a
3
-sphere with radius
r_{\!\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}
as its minimum with
\breve{T}_{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}\!=\!O(4)_{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}
. We obtained
\mathcal{V}_{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}
(
225
), which is the general renormalizable potential of
\rho_{\mathrm{p}}
, by adding the terms
({\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!\!2\alpha})^{2}
and
({\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!\!2\delta})^{2}
to
\breve{\mathcal{V}}_{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}
(
223
). The newly added terms break
\breve{T}_{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}
to
\acute{G}_{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}\!=\!((U(1)\!\times\!Z_{2})\rtimes K_{4})_{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}
generated by the group actions
({\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}_{1},{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}_{2})^{\scriptscriptstyle T}\rightarrow(e^{i\theta}{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}_{1},e^{-i\theta}{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}_{2})^{\scriptscriptstyle T}
,
({\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}_{1},{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}_{2})^{\scriptscriptstyle T}\rightarrow(-{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}_{1},{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}_{2})^{\scriptscriptstyle T}
,
({\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}_{1},{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}_{2})^{\scriptscriptstyle T}\rightarrow({\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}_{2},{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}_{1})^{\scriptscriptstyle T}
and
({\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}_{1},{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}_{2})^{\scriptscriptstyle T}\rightarrow({\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}_{1}^{*},{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}_{2}^{*})^{\scriptscriptstyle T}
. The
3
-sphere gets stratified under this group. Let us consider the one-dimensional manifold
\frac{r_{\!\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}}{\sqrt{2}}(e^{i\theta},e^{-i\theta})^{\scriptscriptstyle T}
in the
3
-sphere. We can show that this manifold is isolated in its stratum; hence, we are guaranteed to obtain it as a stationary manifold of
\mathcal{V}_{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}
(
225
). The rewritten potential (
226
) shows that it can be obtained as a minimum since it corresponds to
|\rho_{\mathrm{p}}|=r_{\!\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}
,
{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!\!2\alpha}=0
and
{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!\!2\delta}=0
. The symmetry groups associated with the minimum
\frac{r_{\!\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}}{\sqrt{2}}(e^{i\theta},e^{-i\theta})^{\scriptscriptstyle T}
are
\tilde{H}_{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}\!=\!\acute{H}_{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}\!=\!Z_{2\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}
generated by
({\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}_{1},{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}_{2})^{\scriptscriptstyle T}\rightarrow({\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}_{2}^{*},{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}_{1}^{*})^{\scriptscriptstyle T}
and
\tilde{T}_{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}=(U(1)\rtimes Z_{2})_{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}=O(2)_{\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}
generated by

11
. Therefore, the only allowed combination where
\phi_{\mathrm{a}}
or
\xi_{\mathrm{c}}
can appear linearly is
\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\otimes\rho_{\mathrm{p}}\otimes\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\otimes\rho_{\mathrm{s}}
. We have already studied this combination, (
212
), (
211
). The only flavons that transform non-trivially under
\mathbf{X}_{\mathrm{\rho}}
and
\mathbf{X}_{\mathrm{\lambda}}
are
\rho\!=\!(\rho_{\mathrm{p}},\rho_{\mathrm{q}},\rho_{\mathrm{r}},\rho_{\mathrm{s}})^{\scriptscriptstyle T}
and
\lambda\!=\!(\lambda_{\mathrm{p}},\lambda_{\mathrm{q}},\lambda_{\mathrm{r}})^{\scriptscriptstyle T}
, respectively. This leads us to conclude that, besides
\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\otimes\rho_{\mathrm{p}}\otimes\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\otimes\rho_{\mathrm{s}}
, the only allowed cross combinations among
\phi_{\mathrm{a}}
,
\rho
and
\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda
are the ones where they contribute quadratically, i.e.,
\{\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\otimes\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\}
,
\{\rho\otimes\rho\}
and
\{\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\otimes\xi_{\mathrm{c}},\lambda\otimes\lambda\}
. The irreps obtained from these quadratic tensor products can be found in TABLES
5
,
14
,
15
. By examining them, we can see that there is only one allowed cross term given by
\rho_{\mathrm{r}}^{2\alpha}\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}^{\scriptscriptstyle T}\lambda_{\mathrm{r}}
. According to corollary B in
Krishnan 2023
, this term is compatible since both
\rho_{\mathrm{r}}^{2\alpha}
and
\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}^{\scriptscriptstyle T}\lambda_{\mathrm{r}}
vanish at our assigned minimum
\langle\rho_{\mathrm{r}}\rangle
(
234
),
\langle\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}\rangle
(
181
),
\langle\lambda_{\mathrm{r}}\rangle
(
183
). Besides this term, we also have cross terms that are products of norms. Among
\phi_{\mathrm{a}}
,
\rho
and
\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda
, we have a total of
4+4+16=24
such cross terms:
|\phi_{\mathrm{a}}|^{2}|\rho_{\mathrm{p}}|^{2}
,
..
,
|\rho_{\mathrm{s}}|^{2}|\lambda_{\mathrm{r}}|^{2}
. They are also compatible. Therefore, adding these cross terms will not spoil the symmetry group
\tilde{H}_{\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rho\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda}
. With this information, we construct the general renormalizable potential

\displaystyle\begin{split}\text{\small$\mathcal{V}_{\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rho\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda}$}&\text{\small$=\tilde{\mathcal{V}}_{\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rho\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda}+c_{102}\rho_{\mathrm{r}}^{2\alpha}\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}^{\scriptscriptstyle T}\lambda_{\mathrm{r}}$}\\
&\text{\small$\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 9.24994pt+c_{103}|\phi_{\mathrm{a}}|^{2}|\rho_{\mathrm{p}}|^{2}+...+c_{126}|\rho_{\mathrm{s}}|^{2}|\lambda_{\mathrm{r}}|^{2}$}\end{split}

(259)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\text{\small$=\tilde{\mathcal{V}}_{\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rho\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda}+k_{84}(\rho_{\mathrm{r}}^{2\alpha}-\frac{r_{\!\rho_{\mathrm{r}}}^{2}}{r_{\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}}r_{\lambda_{\mathrm{r}}}}\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}^{\scriptscriptstyle T}\lambda_{\mathrm{r}})^{2}+$}\\
&\text{\small$\hskip 9.24994pt+k_{85}(|{\phi_{\mathrm{a}}}|^{2}\!-\!\frac{r_{\!\phi_{\mathrm{a}}}^{2}}{r_{\!\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}^{2}}|\rho_{\mathrm{p}}|^{2})^{2}\!+...+k_{108}(|{\rho_{\mathrm{s}}}|^{2}\!-\!\frac{r_{\!\rho_{\mathrm{s}}}^{2}}{r_{\!\lambda_{\mathrm{r}}}^{2}}|\lambda_{\mathrm{r}}|^{2})^{2}$}\!.\end{split}

(260)

This potential has a discrete minimum at our assigned vevs:
\langle\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rangle
(
.3
),
\langle\rho_{\mathrm{p}}\rangle
(
216
),
\langle\rho_{\mathrm{q}}\rangle
(
227
),
\langle\rho_{\mathrm{r}}\rangle
(
234
),
\langle\rho_{\mathrm{s}}\rangle
(
216
),
\langle\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\rangle
(
.3
),
\langle\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}\rangle
(
181
),
\langle\lambda_{\mathrm{q}}\rangle
(
182
),
\langle\lambda_{\mathrm{r}}\rangle
(
183
) with
\tilde{H}_{\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rho\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda}=(K_{4}\rtimes Z_{2})_{\phi_{\mathrm{a}}}\times K_{4\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}\times Z_{2\rho_{\mathrm{q}}}\times Z_{2\rho_{\mathrm{r}}}\times Z_{2\rho_{\mathrm{s}}}\times K_{4\xi_{\mathrm{c}}}\times(S_{4}\times Z_{2})_{\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}}\times(S_{4}\times Z_{2})_{\lambda_{\mathrm{q}}}\times((Z_{2_{1}}\times Z_{2_{3}})\rtimes Z_{2})_{\lambda_{\mathrm{r}}}
. (
260
) contains
74
arbitrary constants
r_{\!\phi_{\mathrm{a}}},r_{\!\rho_{\mathrm{p}}},r_{\!\rho_{\mathrm{q}}},r_{\!\rho_{\mathrm{r}}},r_{\!\rho_{\mathrm{s}}},r_{\!\xi_{\mathrm{c}}},r_{\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}},r_{\lambda_{\mathrm{q}}},r_{\lambda_{\mathrm{r}}},k_{44},..,k_{108}
, which are functions of the
74
coefficients
c_{53},..,c_{126}
in (
259
).

D.7
The potential involving
\phi_{\mathrm{c}}

Construction for the potential of
\phi_{\mathrm{c}}
follows steps very similar to that of
\phi_{\mathrm{a}}
. Using
\phi_{\mathrm{c}}
, we construct

\mathcal{V}_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}}

=c_{127}\,m^{2}|\phi_{\mathrm{c}}|^{2}+c_{128}|\phi_{\mathrm{c}}|^{4}

(261)

=k_{109}(|\phi_{\mathrm{c}}|^{2}-r_{\!\phi_{\mathrm{c}}}^{2})^{2}-k_{109}r_{\!\phi_{\mathrm{c}}}^{4}.

(262)

It has a
3
-sphere as its minimum with
\tilde{T}_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}}=O(4)_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}}
. To break
\tilde{T}_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}}
, we introduce the auxiliary generator
\mathbf{X}_{\mathrm{\eta}}
and driving flavons
\eta_{\mathrm{p}}=({\eta_{\mathrm{p}}}_{1},{\eta_{\mathrm{p}}}_{2})^{\scriptscriptstyle T}
,
\eta_{\mathrm{q}}
,
\eta_{\mathrm{r}}
and
\eta_{\mathrm{s}}
, which transform as given in TABLE
16
. We assign the following vevs for these flavons,

\displaystyle\langle\eta_{\mathrm{p}}\rangle

\displaystyle=\frac{r_{\!\eta_{\mathrm{p}}}}{\sqrt{2}}(\bar{\varsigma},\varsigma)^{\scriptscriptstyle T},

\displaystyle\langle\eta_{\mathrm{q}}\rangle

\displaystyle=r_{\!\eta_{\mathrm{q}}}\tau,

(263)

\displaystyle\langle\eta_{\mathrm{r}}\rangle

\displaystyle=-r_{\!\eta_{\mathrm{r}}}\bar{\tau},

\displaystyle\langle\eta_{\mathrm{s}}\rangle

\displaystyle=-r_{\!\eta_{\mathrm{s}}}i.

(264)

The stabilizers of these vevs are provided in TABLE
10
. These stabilizers fully determine the corresponding vevs up to their norms. Hence, the vevs are guaranteed to be stationary points of the corresponding potentials.

\displaystyle={\phi_{\mathrm{c\eta}}}^{\!\!\!\!\!\!2\alpha}\oplus{\phi_{\mathrm{c\eta}}}^{\!\!\!\!\!\!2\beta}\oplus{\phi_{\mathrm{c\eta}}}^{\!\!\!\!\!\!2\gamma},

\displaystyle=\!|\eta_{\mathrm{p}}|^{2}\!\oplus\!{\eta_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!\!2\alpha}\!\oplus\!{\eta_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!\!2\beta}\!\oplus\!{\eta_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!\!2\gamma}\!\oplus\!{\eta_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!\!2\delta}\oplus\!{\eta_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!\!2\epsilon}\oplus\!{\eta_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!\!2\zeta},\!\!\!\!

(269)

where

\displaystyle{\phi_{\mathrm{c\eta}}}^{\!\!\!\!\!\!2\alpha}

\displaystyle\!-{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}}_{1}^{\!\!*}{\eta_{\mathrm{p}}}_{1})^{\scriptscriptstyle T},

with

Using the same procedure as followed in the previous section, we can show that
\mathcal{V}_{\eta}
has a discrete minimum at (
263
), (
264
). The associated symmetry groups are
\tilde{H}_{\eta}=K_{4\eta_{\mathrm{p}}}\times Z_{2\eta_{\mathrm{q}}}\times Z_{2\eta_{\mathrm{r}}}\times Z_{2\eta_{\mathrm{s}}}

.

) contains

arbitrary constants

.

We combine
\mathcal{V}_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}}
,
\mathcal{V}_{\eta}
and

\text{\small$\breve{\mathcal{V}}_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}\eta\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rho\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda}=\mathcal{V}_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}}+\mathcal{V}_{\eta}+\mathcal{V}_{\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rho\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda}$}.

(307)

This potential has a minimum corresponding to a
3
-sphere for
\phi_{\mathrm{c}}
and the vevs
\langle\eta_{\mathrm{p}}\rangle
,
\langle\eta_{\mathrm{q}}\rangle
,
\langle\eta_{\mathrm{r}}\rangle
,

,
\langle\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rangle
,
\langle\rho_{\mathrm{p}}\rangle
,
\langle\rho_{\mathrm{q}}\rangle
,
\langle\rho_{\mathrm{r}}\rangle
,
\langle\rho_{\mathrm{s}}\rangle
,
\langle\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\rangle
,
\langle\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}\rangle
,
\langle\lambda_{\mathrm{q}}\rangle
and
\langle\lambda_{\mathrm{r}}\rangle
for the rest of the flavons. The minimum has the symmetry groups
\breve{H}_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}\eta\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rho\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda}=\tilde{H}_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}}\times\tilde{H}_{\eta}\times\tilde{H}_{\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rho\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda}=K_{4\eta_{\mathrm{p}}}\times Z_{2\eta_{\mathrm{q}}}\times Z_{2\eta_{\mathrm{r}}}\times Z_{2\eta_{\mathrm{s}}}\times(K_{4}\rtimes Z_{2})_{\phi_{\mathrm{a}}}\times K_{4\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}\times Z_{2\rho_{\mathrm{q}}}\times Z_{2\rho_{\mathrm{r}}}\times Z_{2\rho_{\mathrm{s}}}\times K_{4\xi_{\mathrm{c}}}\times(S_{4}\times Z_{2})_{\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}}\times(S_{4}\times Z_{2})_{\lambda_{\mathrm{q}}}\times((Z_{2_{1}}\times Z_{2_{3}})\rtimes Z_{2})_{\lambda_{\mathrm{r}}}
and
\breve{T}_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}\eta\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rho\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda}=\tilde{T}_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}}\times\tilde{T}_{\eta}\times\tilde{T}_{\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rho\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda}=O(4)_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}}
. We use the cross term between
\phi_{\mathrm{c}}
and
\xi_{\mathrm{c}}
to break
O(4)_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}}
,

\tilde{\mathcal{V}}_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}\eta\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rho\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda}

=\breve{\mathcal{V}}_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}\eta\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rho\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda}+c_{149}\,\text{re}({\phi_{\mathrm{c\eta}}}^{\!\!\!\!\!\!2\alpha\dagger}{\xi_{\mathrm{c\eta}}}^{\!\!\!\!\!\!2\alpha})

(308)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\qquad\quad\,\,\,\,\,&\text{\small$=\breve{\mathcal{V}}_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}\eta\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rho\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda}+k_{126}|{\phi_{\mathrm{c\eta}}}^{\!\!\!\!\!\!2\alpha}-\frac{r_{\!\phi_{\mathrm{c}}}r_{\!\eta_{\mathrm{p}}}}{r_{\!\xi_{\mathrm{c}}}r_{\!\eta_{\mathrm{s}}}}{\xi_{\mathrm{c\eta}}}^{\!\!\!\!\!\!2\alpha}|^{2}$}\\
&\text{\small$\hskip 18.49988pt+\frac{k_{126}}{2}\frac{r_{\!\eta_{\mathrm{p}}}^{2}}{r_{\!\phi_{\mathrm{c}}}^{2}}(|{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}}|^{2}-\frac{r_{\!\phi_{\mathrm{c}}}^{2}}{r_{\!\eta_{\mathrm{p}}}^{2}}|\eta_{\mathrm{p}}|^{2})^{2}$}\\
&\text{\small$\hskip 18.49988pt+\frac{k_{126}}{2}\frac{r_{\!\phi_{\mathrm{c}}}^{2}r_{\!\eta_{\mathrm{p}}}^{2}}{r_{\!\xi_{\mathrm{c}}}^{4}}(|{\xi_{\mathrm{c}}}|^{2}-\frac{r_{\!\xi_{\mathrm{c}}}^{2}}{r_{\!\eta_{\mathrm{s}}}^{2}}|\eta_{\mathrm{s}}|^{2})^{2}$}.\end{split}

(309)

The newly added term
\text{re}({\phi_{\mathrm{c\eta}}}^{\!\!\!\!\!\!2\alpha\dagger}{\xi_{\mathrm{c\eta}}}^{\!\!\!\!\!\!2\alpha})
(
308
) breaks
\breve{T}_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}\eta\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rho\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda}=O(4)_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}}
to
\acute{G}_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}\eta\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rho\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda}=(K_{4}\rtimes Z_{2})_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}}
generated by
{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}}_{2}\rightarrow{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}}_{2}^{*}
,
{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}}_{1}\rightarrow-i{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}}_{1}^{*}
and
\phi_{\mathrm{c}}\rightarrow\boldsymbol{\tau}_{2}\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{\!x}\phi_{\mathrm{c}}
. The
3
-sphere gets stratified under
(K_{4}\rtimes Z_{2})_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}}
. The stabilizer of
\langle\phi_{\mathrm{c}}\rangle
(
.3
) is
(K_{4}\rtimes Z_{2})_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}}
itself, i.e., we have
\acute{H}_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}\eta\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rho\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda}=(K_{4}\rtimes Z_{2})_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}}
. It fully determines
\langle\phi_{\mathrm{c}}\rangle
in the
3

\tilde{\mathcal{V}}_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}\eta\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rho\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda}
is guaranteed to have a stationary point corresponding to
\langle\phi_{\mathrm{c}}\rangle
and the vevs of the other flavons. The associated symmetry groups are
\tilde{H}_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}\eta\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rho\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda}=\acute{H}_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}\eta\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rho\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda}\times\breve{H}_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}\eta\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rho\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda}=(K_{4}\rtimes Z_{2})_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}}\times K_{4\eta_{\mathrm{p}}}\times Z_{2\eta_{\mathrm{q}}}\times Z_{2\eta_{\mathrm{r}}}\times Z_{2\eta_{\mathrm{s}}}\times(K_{4}\rtimes Z_{2})_{\phi_{\mathrm{a}}}\times K_{4\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}\times Z_{2\rho_{\mathrm{q}}}\times Z_{2\rho_{\mathrm{r}}}\times Z_{2\rho_{\mathrm{s}}}\times K_{4\xi_{\mathrm{c}}}\times(S_{4}\times Z_{2})_{\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}}\times(S_{4}\times Z_{2})_{\lambda_{\mathrm{q}}}\times((Z_{2_{1}}\times Z_{2_{3}})\rtimes Z_{2})_{\lambda_{\mathrm{r}}}
and
\tilde{T}_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}\eta\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rho\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda}=1
. The rewritten form of the potential (

) shows that the stationary point can be obtained as a minimum. (
308
) and (
309
) contain

c_{53},..,c_{149}
and
97
arbitrary constants
r_{\!\xi_{\mathrm{c}}},..,r_{\!\eta_{\mathrm{s}}},k_{44},..,k_{126}
, respectively.

To construct the general potential involving these flavons, we need to include various cross terms that we have not yet considered. To this end, we consider them as three multiplets:
\phi_{\mathrm{c}}
,
\eta=(\eta_{\mathrm{p}},\eta_{\mathrm{q}},\eta_{\mathrm{r}},\eta_{\mathrm{s}})^{\scriptscriptstyle T}
and
\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rho\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda=(\phi_{\mathrm{a}},\rho_{\mathrm{p}},\rho_{\mathrm{q}},\rho_{\mathrm{r}},\rho_{\mathrm{s}},\xi_{\mathrm{c}},\lambda_{\mathrm{p}},\lambda_{\mathrm{q}},\lambda_{\mathrm{r}})^{\scriptscriptstyle T}
, and search for the cross terms among these multiplets. Under
\mathbf{E}_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}}
,
\mathbf{E}_{\eta}
,
\mathbf{E}_{\phi_{\mathrm{a}}}
,
\mathbf{E}_{\rho}
and
\mathbf{E}_{\xi_{\mathrm{c}}}
, TABLE
11
, the flavons
\{\phi_{\mathrm{c}},\eta_{\mathrm{p}}\}
,
\{\eta_{\mathrm{p}},\eta_{\mathrm{s}}\}
,
\{\phi_{\mathrm{a}},\rho_{\mathrm{p}}\}
,
\{\rho_{\mathrm{p}},\rho_{\mathrm{s}}\}
and
\{\xi_{\mathrm{c}},\rho_{\mathrm{p}},\eta_{\mathrm{p}}\}
, respectively, change sign while every other flavon remains invariant. This leads us to conclude that the only combinations where
\phi_{\mathrm{c}}
,
\phi_{\mathrm{a}}
and/or
\xi_{\mathrm{c}}
can appear linearly are

and

. We already analyzed these products earlier in this Section and in Section

. Other than these two cases, every possible cross term among our three multiplets can involve quadratic contributions only from every type of flavon, i.e.,
\{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}\otimes\phi_{\mathrm{c}}\}
,
\{\eta\otimes\eta\}
and
\{\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\otimes\phi_{\mathrm{a}},\rho\otimes\rho,\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\otimes\xi_{\mathrm{c}},\lambda\otimes\lambda\}
. How the irreps obtained from these tensor products transform is given in TABLES
5
,
14
,
15
,
16
. From examining them, we find that there exist only four cross terms:
\eta_{\mathrm{r}}^{2\alpha}\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}^{\scriptscriptstyle T}\lambda_{\mathrm{r}}
,

,
\rho_{\mathrm{r}}^{2\beta}\eta_{\mathrm{p}}^{2\gamma}
and

.

The irreps
\eta_{\mathrm{r}}^{2\alpha}
,

and

vanish at our assigned vevs
\langle\eta_{\mathrm{r}}\rangle
(

),

(

),

(

), and

(

\eta_{\mathrm{r}}^{2\alpha}\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}^{\scriptscriptstyle T}\lambda_{\mathrm{r}}
and

are compatible with
\tilde{H}_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}\eta\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rho\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda}
. To verify the compatibility of the term
\rho_{\mathrm{r}}^{2\beta}\eta_{\mathrm{p}}^{2\gamma}
, we investigate how
\tilde{H}_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}\eta\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rho\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda}
acts on
\rho_{\mathrm{r}}^{2\beta}
and
\eta_{\mathrm{p}}^{2\gamma}
. This corresponds to the actions of

and

on

and

, respectively, which turn out to be trivial. In other words, the representations of
\tilde{H}_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}\eta\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rho\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda}
acting on both
\rho_{\mathrm{r}}^{2\beta}
and
\eta_{\mathrm{p}}^{2\gamma}
are
\{1\}
. According to corollary A in
Krishnan 2023
, if the representations of the symmetry group acting on two multiplets are equal, then the term obtained as their product is compatible with the group. Therefore, the term
\rho_{\mathrm{r}}^{2\beta}\eta_{\mathrm{p}}^{2\gamma}
is compatible. Similarly, we can show that the representations of
\tilde{H}_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}\eta\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rho\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda}
acting on both
\rho_{\mathrm{p}}^{2\gamma}
and
\eta_{\mathrm{r}}^{2\beta}
are
\{1\}
; hence, the term
\rho_{\mathrm{p}}^{2\gamma}\eta_{\mathrm{r}}^{2\beta}
is compatible. Besides these cross terms, we have
4+9+36=49
products of norms, i.e.,
|\phi_{\mathrm{c}}|^{2}|\eta_{\mathrm{p}}|^{2},..,|\eta_{\mathrm{s}}|^{2}|\lambda_{\mathrm{r}}|^{2}
. They also are compatible. We include all these cross terms to construct the general potential,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\text{\small$\mathcal{V}_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}\eta\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rho\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda}$}&\text{\small$=\tilde{\mathcal{V}}_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}\eta\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rho\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda}+c_{150}\eta_{\mathrm{r}}^{2\alpha}\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}^{\scriptscriptstyle T}\lambda_{\mathrm{r}}+c_{151}\rho_{\mathrm{r}}^{2\alpha}\eta_{\mathrm{r}}^{2\alpha}$}\\
&\text{\small$\hskip 9.24994pt+c_{152}\rho_{\mathrm{r}}^{2\beta}\eta_{\mathrm{p}}^{2\gamma}\!+c_{153}\rho_{\mathrm{p}}^{2\gamma}\eta_{\mathrm{r}}^{2\beta}\!+c_{154}|\phi_{\mathrm{c}}|^{2}|\eta_{\mathrm{p}}|^{2}\,\,\,\,\,\,$}\\
&\text{\small$\hskip 9.24994pt+...+c_{202}|\eta_{\mathrm{s}}|^{2}|\lambda_{\mathrm{r}}|^{2}$}\end{split}

(310)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\qquad\quad\,\,\,\,\,&\text{\small$=\tilde{\mathcal{V}}_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}\eta\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rho\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda}+k_{127}(\eta_{\mathrm{r}}^{2\alpha}-\frac{r_{\!\eta_{\mathrm{r}}}^{2}}{r_{\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}}r_{\lambda_{\mathrm{r}}}}\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}^{\scriptscriptstyle T}\lambda_{\mathrm{r}})^{2}$}\\
&\text{\small$\hskip 9.24994pt+k_{128}(\rho_{\mathrm{r}}^{2\alpha}-\frac{r_{\!\rho_{\mathrm{r}}}^{2}}{r_{\!\eta_{\mathrm{r}}}^{2}}\eta_{\mathrm{r}}^{2\alpha})^{2}+k_{129}(\rho_{\mathrm{r}}^{2\beta}-\frac{2r_{\!\rho_{\mathrm{r}}}^{2}}{r_{\!\eta_{\mathrm{p}}}^{2}}\eta_{\mathrm{p}}^{2\gamma})^{2}\!\!\!$}\\
&\text{\small$\hskip 9.24994pt+k_{130}(\rho_{\mathrm{p}}^{2\gamma}-\frac{r_{\!\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}^{2}}{2r_{\!\eta_{\mathrm{r}}}^{2}}\eta_{\mathrm{r}}^{2\beta})^{2}+k_{131}(|{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}}|^{2}\!-\!\frac{r_{\!\phi_{\mathrm{c}}}^{2}}{r_{\!\eta_{\mathrm{p}}}^{2}}|\eta_{\mathrm{p}}|^{2})^{2}$}\\
&\text{\small$\hskip 9.24994pt+...+k_{179}(|{\eta_{\mathrm{s}}}|^{2}-\frac{r_{\!\eta_{\mathrm{s}}}^{2}}{r_{\!\lambda_{\mathrm{r}}}^{2}}|\lambda_{\mathrm{r}}|^{2})^{2}$}.\end{split}

(311)

Since the newly added cross terms are compatible with
\tilde{H}_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}\eta\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rho\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda}

\mathcal{V}_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}\eta\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rho\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda}
(

) and (

) contain
150
coefficients and
150
arbitrary constants, respectively.

D.8
The potential involving all the flavons

In the model, we introduced
7
flavons,
\chi
,
\xi_{\mathrm{a}}
,
\xi_{\mathrm{b}}
,
\xi_{\mathrm{c}}
,
\phi_{\mathrm{a}}
,
\phi_{\mathrm{b}}
,
\phi_{\mathrm{c}}
, that appear in the fermion mass matrices and
16
driving flavons,
\mu_{\mathrm{p}}
,
\mu_{\mathrm{q}}
,
\nu_{\mathrm{p}}
,
\nu_{\mathrm{q}}
,
\nu_{\mathrm{r}}
,
\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}
,
\lambda_{\mathrm{q}}
,
\lambda_{\mathrm{r}}
,
\rho_{\mathrm{p}}
,
\rho_{\mathrm{q}}
,
\rho_{\mathrm{r}}
,
\rho_{\mathrm{s}}
,
\eta_{\mathrm{p}}
,
\eta_{\mathrm{q}}
,
\eta_{\mathrm{r}}
,
\eta_{\mathrm{s}}
. By involving these
23
flavons, we construct

\text{\small$\tilde{\mathcal{V}}=\mathcal{V}_{\chi}+\mathcal{V}_{\xi_{\mathrm{a}}}+\mathcal{V}_{\xi_{\mathrm{b}}\mu}+\mathcal{V}_{\phi_{\mathrm{b}}\nu}+\mathcal{V}_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}\eta\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rho\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda}$}.

(312)

This potential has a discrete minimum that corresponds to the various vevs we previously assigned to the flavons, i.e.,

\begin{split}&\langle\chi\rangle,\langle\xi_{\mathrm{a}}\rangle,\langle\xi_{\mathrm{b}}\rangle,\langle\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\rangle,\langle\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rangle,\langle\phi_{\mathrm{b}}\rangle,\langle\phi_{\mathrm{c}}\rangle,\quad\text{(\ref{eq:elemvevs})}\\
&\langle\mu_{\mathrm{p}}\rangle,\langle\mu_{\mathrm{q}}\rangle,\quad\text{(\ref{eq:mupvev}), (\ref{eq:muqvev})}\\
&\langle\nu_{\mathrm{p}}\rangle,\langle\nu_{\mathrm{q}}\rangle,\langle\nu_{\mathrm{r}}\rangle,\quad\text{(\ref{eq:nuprvevs}), (\ref{eq:nuqvev})}\\
&\langle\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}\rangle,\langle\lambda_{\mathrm{q}}\rangle,\langle\lambda_{\mathrm{r}}\rangle,\quad\text{(\ref{eq:lampvev})-(\ref{eq:lamrvev})}\\
&\langle\rho_{\mathrm{p}}\rangle,\langle\rho_{\mathrm{q}}\rangle,\langle\rho_{\mathrm{r}}\rangle,\langle\rho_{\mathrm{s}}\rangle,\quad\text{(\ref{eq:rhopsvevs}), (\ref{eq:rhoqvev}), (\ref{eq:rhorvev})}\\
&\langle\eta_{\mathrm{p}}\rangle,\langle\eta_{\mathrm{q}}\rangle,\langle\eta_{\mathrm{r}}\rangle,\langle\eta_{\mathrm{s}}\rangle.\quad\text{(\ref{eq:etapqvevs}), (\ref{eq:etarsvevs})}\end{split}

(313)

The symmetry group associated with this minimum is

\begin{split}\tilde{H}&=\tilde{H}_{\chi}\times\tilde{H}_{\xi_{\mathrm{a}}}\times\tilde{H}_{\xi_{\mathrm{b}}\mu}\times\tilde{H}_{\phi_{\mathrm{b}}\nu}\times\tilde{H}_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}\eta\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rho\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda}\\
&=S_{3\chi}\times Z_{2\xi_{\mathrm{a}}}\times(K_{4}\rtimes Z_{2})_{\xi_{\mathrm{b}}}\times(K_{4}\rtimes Z_{2})_{\mu_{\mathrm{p}}}\times Z_{2\mu_{\mathrm{q}}}\\
&\quad\times(K_{4}\rtimes Z_{2})_{\phi_{\mathrm{b}}}\times(K_{4}\rtimes Z_{2})_{\nu_{\mathrm{p}}}\times Z_{2\nu_{\mathrm{q}}}\times Z_{2\nu_{\mathrm{r}}}\\
&\quad\times(K_{4}\rtimes Z_{2})_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}}\times K_{4\eta_{\mathrm{p}}}\times Z_{2\eta_{\mathrm{q}}}\times Z_{2\eta_{\mathrm{r}}}\times Z_{2\eta_{\mathrm{s}}}\\
&\quad\times(K_{4}\rtimes Z_{2})_{\phi_{\mathrm{a}}}\times K_{4\rho_{\mathrm{p}}}\times Z_{2\rho_{\mathrm{q}}}\times Z_{2\rho_{\mathrm{r}}}\times Z_{2\rho_{\mathrm{s}}}\times K_{4\xi_{\mathrm{c}}}\\
&\quad\times(S_{4}\times Z_{2})_{\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}}\times(S_{4}\times Z_{2})_{\lambda_{\mathrm{q}}}\times((Z_{2_{1}}\times Z_{2_{3}})\rtimes Z_{2})_{\lambda_{\mathrm{r}}}.\end{split}

(314)

\tilde{\mathcal{V}}
(
312
) contains
202
coefficients
c_{1},..,c_{202}
and
202
arbitrary constants
r_{\!\chi},..,r_{\!\eta_{\mathrm{s}}},k_{1},..,k_{179}
.

To construct the general potential involving all the flavons, we consider them as five multiplets:
\chi
,
\xi_{\mathrm{a}}
,
\xi_{\mathrm{b}}\mu=(\xi_{\mathrm{b}},\mu_{\mathrm{p}},\mu_{\mathrm{q}})^{\scriptscriptstyle T}
,
\phi_{\mathrm{b}}\nu=(\phi_{\mathrm{b}},\nu_{\mathrm{p}},\nu_{\mathrm{q}},\nu_{\mathrm{r}})^{\scriptscriptstyle T}
and
\phi_{\mathrm{c}}\eta\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\rho\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\lambda=(\phi_{\mathrm{c}},\eta_{\mathrm{p}},\eta_{\mathrm{q}},\eta_{\mathrm{r}},\eta_{\mathrm{s}},\phi_{\mathrm{a}},\rho_{\mathrm{p}},\rho_{\mathrm{q}},\rho_{\mathrm{r}},\rho_{\mathrm{s}},\xi_{\mathrm{c}},\lambda_{\mathrm{p}},\lambda_{\mathrm{q}},\lambda_{\mathrm{r}})^{\scriptscriptstyle T}
. Let us search for the cross terms among these five multiplets. Flavons in each multiplet, with the exception of
\xi_{\mathrm{a}}
, transform under one or more generators whose action is trivial on the flavons in the other multiplets. Therefore, no flavon, with the possible exception of
\xi_{\mathrm{a}}
, can couple linearly with the flavons in another multiplet. The flavons that transform nontrivially under
\mathbf{E}_{\xi_{\mathrm{a}}}
, TABLE
11
, are
\xi_{\mathrm{a}}
,
\xi_{\mathrm{b}}
and
\mu_{\mathrm{p}}
only. This implies that
\xi_{\mathrm{a}}
can couple linearly with
\xi_{\mathrm{b}}
and/or
\mu_{\mathrm{p}}
only. On the other hand,
\xi_{\mathrm{b}}
is the only flavon that changes sign under
\mathbf{E}_{\xi_{\mathrm{b}}}
, and
\mu_{\mathrm{p}}
and
\mu_{\mathrm{q}}
are the only flavons that change sign under
\mathbf{E}_{\mu}
, TABLE
11
. Therefore, the only possible cases of
\xi_{\mathrm{a}}
coupling linearly with flavons in another multiplet are
\xi_{\mathrm{a}}\otimes\xi_{\mathrm{b}}\otimes\xi_{\mathrm{b}}
,
\xi_{\mathrm{a}}\otimes\mu_{\mathrm{p}}\otimes\mu_{\mathrm{p}}
and
\xi_{\mathrm{a}}\otimes\mu_{\mathrm{p}}\otimes\mu_{\mathrm{q}}
. Towards the end of Section
.3
, we studied
\xi_{\mathrm{a}}\otimes\xi_{\mathrm{b}}\otimes\xi_{\mathrm{b}}
and found that no irrep obtained from this tensor product is an invariant. How the various irreps obtained from
\mu_{\mathrm{p}}\otimes\mu_{\mathrm{p}}
and
\mu_{\mathrm{p}}\otimes\mu_{\mathrm{q}}
transform is given in TABLE
9
, which rules out obtaining invariants from
\xi_{\mathrm{a}}\otimes\mu_{\mathrm{p}}\otimes\mu_{\mathrm{p}}
and
\xi_{\mathrm{a}}\otimes\mu_{\mathrm{p}}\otimes\mu_{\mathrm{q}}
also. Therefore, no flavon, including
\xi_{\mathrm{a}}
, can couple linearly with flavons in another multiplet. This would imply that in cross terms, the flavons in the various multiplets can contribute quadratically only, i.e.,
\{\chi\otimes\chi\}
,
\{\xi_{\mathrm{a}}\otimes\xi_{\mathrm{a}}\}
,
\{\xi_{\mathrm{b}}\otimes\xi_{\mathrm{b}},\mu\otimes\mu\}
,
\{\phi_{\mathrm{b}}\otimes\phi_{\mathrm{b}},\nu\otimes\nu\}
and
\{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}\otimes\phi_{\mathrm{c}},\eta\otimes\eta,\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\otimes\phi_{\mathrm{a}},\rho\otimes\rho,\xi_{\mathrm{c}}\otimes\xi_{\mathrm{c}},\lambda\otimes\lambda\}
. How the irreps obtained from these tensor products transform is given in TABLES
5
,
9
,
12
,
14
,
15
,
16
. By examining them, we obtain four cross terms:
{\phi_{\mathrm{b}}}^{\!\!\!\!2\alpha}{\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\!\lambda_{\mathrm{q}}
,
{\nu_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!2\alpha}{\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\!\lambda_{\mathrm{q}}
,
{\nu_{\mathrm{q}}}^{\!\!\!2\alpha}{\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\!\lambda_{\mathrm{q}}
,
{\nu_{\mathrm{r}}}^{\!\!2\alpha}{\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\!\lambda_{\mathrm{q}}
. Since
{\phi_{\mathrm{b}}}^{\!\!\!\!2\alpha}
,
{\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\!\lambda_{\mathrm{q}}
,
{\nu_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!2\alpha}
,
{\nu_{\mathrm{q}}}^{\!\!\!2\alpha}
and
{\nu_{\mathrm{r}}}^{\!\!2\alpha}
vanish at our assigned vev (
313
), these cross terms are compatible with
\tilde{H}
(
314
). We also have cross terms that are products of norms, i.e.,
|\chi|^{2}|\xi_{\mathrm{a}}|^{2}
, ..,
|\nu_{\mathrm{r}}|^{2}|\lambda_{\mathrm{r}}|^{2}
, which consists of a total of
1+3+4+14+3+4+14+12+42+56=153
terms. We include all these cross terms to construct the general renormalizable potential containing the
23
flavons,

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!\text{\small$\mathcal{V}$}

\displaystyle\text{\small$=\tilde{\mathcal{V}}+c_{203}{\phi_{\mathrm{b}}}^{\!\!\!\!2\alpha}{\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\!\lambda_{\mathrm{q}}+c_{204}{\nu_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!2\alpha}{\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\!\lambda_{\mathrm{q}}+c_{205}{\nu_{\mathrm{q}}}^{\!\!\!2\alpha}{\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\!\lambda_{\mathrm{q}}$}

(315)

\displaystyle\text{\small$\hskip 9.24994pt+c_{206}{\nu_{\mathrm{r}}}^{\!\!2\alpha}{\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\!\lambda_{\mathrm{q}}+c_{207}|\chi|^{2}|\xi_{\mathrm{a}}|^{2}+...+c_{359}|\nu_{\mathrm{r}}|^{2}|\eta_{\mathrm{s}}|^{2}$}\!\!\!\!

\displaystyle

\displaystyle\text{\small$=\tilde{\mathcal{V}}+k_{180}({\phi_{\mathrm{b}}}^{\!\!\!\!2\alpha}-\frac{r_{\!\phi_{\mathrm{b}}}^{2}}{r_{\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}}r_{\lambda_{\mathrm{q}}}}{\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\!\lambda_{\mathrm{q}})^{2}+k_{181}({\nu_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!2\alpha}-\frac{r_{\!\nu_{\mathrm{p}}}^{2}}{r_{\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}}r_{\lambda_{\mathrm{q}}}}{\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\!\lambda_{\mathrm{q}})^{2}$}

(316)

\displaystyle\text{\small$\hskip 9.24994pt+k_{182}({\nu_{\mathrm{q}}}^{\!\!\!2\alpha}-\frac{r_{\!\nu_{\mathrm{q}}}^{2}}{r_{\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}}r_{\lambda_{\mathrm{q}}}}{\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\!\lambda_{\mathrm{q}})^{2}+k_{183}({\nu_{\mathrm{r}}}^{\!\!2\alpha}-\frac{r_{\!\nu_{\mathrm{r}}}^{2}}{r_{\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}}r_{\lambda_{\mathrm{q}}}}{\lambda_{\mathrm{p}}}^{\!\!\!{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\!\lambda_{\mathrm{q}})^{2}$}

\displaystyle\text{\small$\hskip 9.24994pt+k_{184}(|\chi|^{2}\!-\!\frac{v_{\chi}^{2}}{r_{\!\xi_{\mathrm{a}}}^{2}}|\xi_{\mathrm{a}}|^{2})^{2}\!+...+\!k_{336}(|{\nu_{\mathrm{r}}}|^{2}\!-\!\frac{r_{\!\nu_{\mathrm{r}}}^{2}}{r_{\lambda_{\mathrm{r}}}^{2}}|\lambda_{\mathrm{r}}|^{2})^{2}.\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!$}

Since the newly added cross terms are compatible,
\tilde{H}
(
314
) remains unaffected, and our vev (
313
) is obtained as a minimum of
\mathcal{V}
(
315
). The rewritten potential (
316
), which makes the minimum manifest, contains
359
arbitrary constants, consisting of the norms of the
23
flavons and
k_{1},...,k_{336}
. They are functions of the
359
coefficients
c_{1},...,c_{359}
present in (
315
).

In this Appendix, we introduced several auxiliary generators beyond
\mathbf{X}
. This enlarges
G_{\!f}
and
G_{x}
compared to their definitions provided in Section
.2.1
. We may find the large number of driving flavons and the corresponding enlargement of the groups aesthetically unappealing. We intend to tackle this issue in future works where we will break the accidental continuous symmetries of the renormalizable flavon potential by utilizing the non-renormalizable terms up to a specific order instead of utilizing the driving flavons. Such an approach would require all the renormalizable cross terms, as well as all the non-renormalizable cross terms up to the specific order, to satisfy the compatibility condition so that they do not spoil our assigned vevs.

Appendix E
Driving flavons and the mass terms

As we saw in Section
.2.2
and TABLE
3
, the combination of flavons that couple with
L^{\dagger}
and
N
to form the Dirac mass term must change sign under
\mathbf{E}_{\xi_{\mathrm{b}}}
and
\mathbf{E}_{\xi_{\mathrm{c}}}
and must remain invariant under
\mathbf{E}_{\xi_{\mathrm{a}}}
. It must also remain invariant under
\mathbf{Z}_{l}
,
\mathbf{E}_{\phi_{\mathrm{a}}}
,
\mathbf{E}_{\phi_{\mathrm{b}}}
,
\mathbf{E}_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}}
,
\mathbf{E}_{\mu}
,
\mathbf{E}_{\nu}
,
\mathbf{E}_{\lambda}
,
\mathbf{E}_{\rho}
and
\mathbf{E}_{\eta}
. From TABLE
11
, we can see that no flavon or pair of flavons that transforms in this way exists. At the cubic order, only one such combination exists, which is nothing but
\xi_{\mathrm{a}}\otimes\xi_{\mathrm{b}}\otimes\xi_{\mathrm{c}}
. The combination that couples with
i\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{\!y}N
and
N
to form the Majorana mass term must change sign under
\mathbf{E}_{\phi_{\mathrm{a}}}
,
\mathbf{E}_{\phi_{\mathrm{b}}}
and
\mathbf{E}_{\phi_{\mathrm{c}}}
and must remain invariant under
\mathbf{Z}_{l}
,
\mathbf{E}_{\xi_{\mathrm{a}}}
,
\mathbf{E}_{\xi_{\mathrm{b}}}
,
\mathbf{E}_{\xi_{\mathrm{c}}}
,
\mathbf{E}_{\mu}
,
\mathbf{E}_{\nu}
,
\mathbf{E}_{\lambda}
,
\mathbf{E}_{\rho}
and
\mathbf{E}_{\eta}
. By examining TABLE
11
, we can show that the cubic product
\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\otimes\phi_{\mathrm{b}}\otimes\phi_{\mathrm{c}}
is the one and only leading order combination that transforms in this way. Thus, we conclude that the driving flavons cannot appear in the mass matrices at the leading order. For a discussion on the tensor product expansions of
\xi_{\mathrm{a}}\otimes\xi_{\mathrm{b}}\otimes\xi_{\mathrm{c}}
and
\phi_{\mathrm{a}}\otimes\phi_{\mathrm{b}}\otimes\phi_{\mathrm{c}}
, please see Appendix
B
.
